# Foundation Changes for Swift

### Foundation

Added CustomNSError.errorCodeAdded CustomNSError.errorDomainAdded CustomNSError.errorUserInfoAdded [Int16.init(_: NSNumber)](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swift/int16/2604010-init)Added [Int32.init(_: NSNumber)](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swift/int32/2604163-init)Added [Int64.init(_: NSNumber)](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swift/int64/2604185-init)Added [Int8.init(_: NSNumber)](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swift/int8/2604176-init)Added [UInt16.init(_: NSNumber)](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swift/uint16/2604054-init)Added [UInt32.init(_: NSNumber)](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swift/uint32/2604217-init)Added [UInt64.init(_: NSNumber)](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swift/uint64/2604047-init)Added [UInt8.init(_: NSNumber)](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swift/uint8/2604116-init)Modified [FileManager](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/foundation/filemanager)
